#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

-        computeForces(bodies);
-        integrateBodies(bodies);
-    }
+    simulateBodies(bodies, numSteps);
     
     auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
     auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;

+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;

+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif

+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;

+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;

-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[lane];
+        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[lane];
+        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        const double* x = storage;
+        const double* y = storage + n;
+        const double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            const double* oldX = x;
+            const double* oldY = y;
+            const double* oldZ = z;
+            x = nextX;
+            y = nextY;
+            z = nextZ;
+            nextX = const_cast<double*>(oldX);
+            nextY = const_cast<double*>(oldY);
+            nextZ = const_cast<double*>(oldZ);
+        }
     }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[lane] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[lane] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // force pass when force calculation and integration are fused.
+        const volatile double deltaVx = DT * Fx[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVy = DT * Fy[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVz = DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.vel.x += deltaVx;
+        body.vel.y += deltaVy;
+        body.vel.z += deltaVz;
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* x = storage;
+        double* y = storage + n;
+        double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* x = storage;
+        double* y = storage + n;
+        double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[lane]);
+            Fy[lane] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[lane]);
+            Fz[lane] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[lane]);
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // force pass when force calculation and integration are fused.
+        const volatile double deltaVx = DT * Fx[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVy = DT * Fy[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVz = DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.vel.x += deltaVx;
+        body.vel.y += deltaVy;
+        body.vel.z += deltaVz;
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* x = storage;
+        double* y = storage + n;
+        double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;

+            Fx[lane] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[lane]);
+            Fy[lane] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[lane]);
+            Fz[lane] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[lane]);

-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // force pass when force calculation and integration are fused.
+        const volatile double deltaVx = DT * Fx[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVy = DT * Fy[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVz = DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.vel.x += deltaVx;
+        body.vel.y += deltaVy;
+        body.vel.z += deltaVz;

+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;

+    }

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* x = storage;
+        double* y = storage + n;
+        double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

fd=os.memfd_create('nbody-scaling',0)
exe='/proc/self/fd/'+str(fd)
subprocess.run(['g++','-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-fno-math-errno','-fopenmp','-Wall','-Wextra','nbody/nbody.cpp','-o',exe,'-lm'],pass_fds=(fd,),check=True)

    for threads in (64,128,256):
        env=dict(os.environ,OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads),OMP_PROC_BIND='close',OMP_PLACES='cores')
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([exe,'-n',str(n),'-s',str(s)],pass_fds=(fd,),env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=15)
            timing=next(line for line in p.stdout.splitlines() if line.startswith('Simulation time:'))
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            timing='over 15 seconds'
        print(json.dumps({'n':n,'steps':s,'threads':threads,'time':timing}),flush=True)
env=dict(os.environ)
for key in ('OMP_NUM_THREADS','OMP_PROC_BIND','OMP_PLACES'):

try:
    p=subprocess.run([exe],pass_fds=(fd,),env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=15)
    print(json.dumps({'default':p.stdout}),flush=True)
except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    print('Default run exceeded 15 seconds',flush=True)

+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-

-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;

-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
+#else
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE = 4;
+#endif
+
+template <size_t Width>
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y, const double* z,
+                      double* nextX, double* nextY, double* nextZ,
+                      size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[Width], py[Width], pz[Width];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Width] = {}, Fy[Width] = {}, Fz[Width] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        px[lane] = x[first + lane];
+        py[lane] = y[first + lane];
+        pz[lane] = z[first + lane];
+    }
+
+    // Vectorize across target bodies, keeping each body's summation in j order.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double sx = x[j], sy = y[j], sz = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+            const double dx = sx - px[lane];
+            const double dy = sy - py[lane];
+            const double dz = sz - pz[lane];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[lane] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[lane]);
+            Fy[lane] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[lane]);
+            Fz[lane] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[lane]);
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    // Only this block's owner writes its velocities and next-step positions.
+    for (size_t lane = 0; lane < Width; ++lane) {
+        Body& body = bodies[first + lane];
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // force pass when force calculation and integration are fused.
+        const volatile double deltaVx = DT * Fx[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVy = DT * Fy[lane];
+        const volatile double deltaVz = DT * Fz[lane];
+        body.vel.x += deltaVx;
+        body.vel.y += deltaVy;
+        body.vel.z += deltaVz;
+        body.pos.x = nextX[first + lane] = px[lane] + body.vel.x * DT;
+        body.pos.y = nextY[first + lane] = py[lane] + body.vel.y * DT;
+        body.pos.z = nextZ[first + lane] = pz[lane] + body.vel.z * DT;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Two compact position snapshots allow force calculation and integration
+    // to share one work loop and one barrier per step.
+    std::vector<double> positions(6 * n);
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
+                                  static_cast<int>(numBlocks));
+    Body* data = bodies.data();
+    double* storage = positions.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) \
+        shared(data, storage, n, numBlocks, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* x = storage;
+        double* y = storage + n;
+        double* z = storage + 2 * n;
+        double* nextX = storage + 3 * n;
+        double* nextY = storage + 4 * n;
+        double* nextZ = storage + 5 * n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            storage[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            storage[n + i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            storage[2 * n + i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+                const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                if (first + FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE <= n) {
+                    advanceBodyBlock<FORCE_BLOCK_SIZE>(data, x, y, z,
+                                                      nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+                } else {
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < n; ++i) {
+                        advanceBodyBlock<1>(data, x, y, z,
+                                            nextX, nextY, nextZ, i, n);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes every next-position write.
+            // Each thread then exchanges its own pointers without another barrier.
+            std::swap(x, nextX);
+            std::swap(y, nextY);
+            std::swap(z, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

+    // Avoid creating large teams for inputs with only a few blocks per worker.
+    const size_t usefulThreads = (numBlocks + 7) / 8;
